Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The activity operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 744. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 174 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the activity space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 744 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 174. QED.
